# Practice Makes Perfect

Journal: `quests/main_quest/prologue/q000_tutorial` · `MainQuest` · hash `2914891760`.

Journal premise: Better to be prepared than dead. The street can be brutally unforgiving, so a quick brush-up on combat skills certainly coudn't hurt.

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 334 exact journal-linked nodes in 6 resources; 7 resources in the inspected dependency context.

Resource roles in that context: 7 content candidate. Role labels describe path/name evidence, not proof of runtime reachability.

Root quest resources with additional journal references: none indexed. Roots remain activation context when a phase/scene anchor exists, to avoid importing sibling launch scaffolding.

Generated structural analysis from decoded resources. Shared resources are context, not exclusive quest ownership. The tables trace stored sockets and fact dependencies; they do not simulate runtime ordering, prove all branches reachable, or infer chronology from journal objective order. World NodeRef definitions/streaming owners, full actor and animation closure, numeric TweakDB names, spoken subtitle/VO payload joins and installed/save behavior have not been validated by this structural pass. ## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

1018 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 48 distinct direct resource identities; 46 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.

NodeRef uses: 926; TweakDB binding uses: 267. Uses retain exact pointers in resource evidence. A global alias alone is not a resolved streaming owner; numeric records are not guessed. See [all-quest analysis](../all-quest-findings.md) for extraction rules and proposed contracts.

## Scene performance events

Section event data retains actor/animation/audio bindings and authored time fields, with raw pointers. Events in different sections are not treated as one chronological timeline: parent routing, interruption and playback policy still apply. Animation/voice payloads and installed timing are not validated by this extraction.

| Scene | Event sections / events | Stored event types |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `base\quest\main_quests\prologue\q000\scenes\q000_vr_tutorial.scene` | 92 / 289 | scnAudioEvent: 6; scnChangeIdleAnimEvent: 13; scnDialogLineEvent: 105; scnGameplayTransitionEvent: 3; scnIKEvent: 6; scnLookAtEvent: 29; scnPlaySkAnimEvent: 51; scnUnmountEvent: 3; scneventsAttachPropToNode: 3; scneventsAttachPropToPerformer: 6; scneventsCameraParamsEvent: 1; scneventsSetAnimFeatureEvent: 13; scneventsSocket: 50 |

Query full events with `tools/query_sidequest_evidence.py --root reference/quests/deep-dives/all-quests --quest <exact title or journal path> --events`; add `--resource`, `--graph scene --node <id>` or `--event-type <substring>` to narrow the output. Scene-global screenplay and interruption data remain in the performance shards.
